Resolver does not assert that semantic reasoning was verified mechanically. |
| ASER-RV-05 | Skills §§3–4: root meta-skill bootstrap has no completed human trust decision; recursion is prohibited but initial root creation/recovery requires independent authority. | First install has no active meta-skill; agent proposes root and attempts to use it to approve itself, or admin accidentally revokes only root and blocks all authoring. | Red, Security, White | HIGH | **Addressed proposal**: manual trusted bootstrap, immutable recovery manifest/receipt, dual-role decision for root changes, fail-closed while absence explicitly visible; source/runtime proof pending. |
| ASER-RV-06 | ASER §2 and Skills §§1–2: manual/policy internal/external classification could mistakenly leak private drafts, or conflate audience and authority. | A public-facing regulatory notice has private operational notes and internal regulator correspondence; public history/diff leaks source attachment because policy `audience=external`. | Purple, Security, Grey | HIGH | **Addressed**: audience, authority origin, distribution, confidentiality and record visibility independent with field/attachment ACL checks; external publication never implies public drafts/history. |
| ASER-RV-07 | Skills §7: instruction injection defense said "reject attempts" but cannot semantically guarantee detection of all malicious skill instructions. | A syntactically valid, human-approved but malicious skill contains indirect exfiltration directions; lint cannot prove safety. | Red, Security, Elephant-Gold | HIGH | **Addressed limit**: provenance/approval, sandboxed rendering, least-privilege tools, external-link policy and human review are primary controls; static checks are imperfect alerts, not a safety proof. |
| ASER-RV-08 | Skills §§5–7 and ASER §6: stale assignment and disabled skill cache; no detailed semantics for active workflows, releases and offline clients. | Human revokes a policy skill after agent fetches it; old client continues creating a published requirement using cached hash. | QA, Security, Graph | HIGH | **Guard specified; implementation unproven**: server-side re-resolution at write/publish, epoch/fingerprint invalidation, no offline promotion with stale binding, immutable old provenance. |
| ASER-RV-09 | ASER requirement/native-case writer and Skills §7: old MCP/REST writer bypass. | Existing `create_testcase` or Zyra save route publishes without carrying governed skill version; new guard lives only in new endpoint. | Blue, QA, Graph | CRITICAL | **Open implementation exit**: inventoried old writers, require shared native enforcement at all entrances; no claim implemented. |
| ASER-RV-10 | ASER Change Packet / R-ASE-30: skill provenance for `files/evidence` can confuse a file's capture method with the authority for observed results. | An uploaded external signed PDF or runtime screenshot has no authoring skill; forced skill pin makes evidence look AI-produced or approved. | Green, QA, Grey | MEDIUM | **Addressed**: record governing skill only when *agent authored/transformed/classified* artifact; neutral externally sourced evidence retains its own source/chain of custody, skill applicability may be N/A with reason. |
| ASER-RV-11 | ASER roadmap S0–S2: rollout and case-approval compatibility require exact source truth and 8 original spec reconciliation. | Proposal declares agent-produced cases always manual-approved while existing per-domain approval mode has bounded auto; development code permits direct saves. | Blue, White, Elephant-Gold | HIGH | **Open owner decision**: promote only after identifying canonical policy modes and routing approved amendments into original eight. |
| ASER-RV-12 | Skills §8 evaluation: self-authored fixtures vs real quality comparison. | Skill v2 passes formatting fixtures authored by same agent but degrades untested security negative-path tests. | QA, Purple, Elephant-Gold | MEDIUM | **Addressed**: independent held-out evaluations, compare false positives/omissions and capability drift; no current actual evaluation results. |
| ASER-RV-13 | Skills §5 composition/prevalence and ASER user jobs: no explicit policy for "no active skill", emergency skill revocation or mandatory skill outage. | An emergency bug fix needs to ship while policy resolver unavailable. An unsafe default permits ungoverned publication; unconditional block prevents emergency repair. | Red, Grey, White, Ops | HIGH | **Owner decision pending**: narrow predefined human-authorized, time-limited emergency exception with full audit and post-review; otherwise fail closed. No agent-created exception. |
| ASER-RV-14 | Skills history/version/audit model: cross-project reusable skill ownership and access rights lack complete lifecycle. | Workspace-global skill changes ownership or is deleted; child project assignment references private inaccessible historical snapshot. | Green, Security, QA | HIGH | **Addressed proposal**: scope/reference permissions, retained immutable head and access revocation, explicit unavailable/invalid assignment, migration and fail-closed history; actual DB proof pending. |
| ASER-RV-15 | Skills UX: authoring skill library, versions, assignments and different audience manuals need end-to-end clear affordances. | Viewer sees apparently active skill but it is inactive in this project; agent creates candidate, reviewer cannot find/approve it; keyboard-only admin cannot activate a new binding. | Purple, Grey/SUJT | MEDIUM | **Design/acceptance added**: distinct candidate, approved, assigned, active, inactive and superseded states; keyboard access, permitted preview, deep link and mutation confirmation. UI/runtime evidence pending. |
| ASER-RV-16 | ASER proposal introduction: "seven domains" turns skills governance into mandatory full assessment of a skill update every change. | Cosmetic code refactor yields a manufactured "no changes" Skills entry; unnecessary work obscures real policy risk. | Green, Red | MEDIUM | **Addressed**: skills disposition is normally "resolved active versions/unchanged", not a fabricated skill proposal; record applicability only when relevant. |
| ASER-RV-17 | ASER graph semantics / Skills §9: "linked" could be conflated with "verified by run" or "approved". | Case references v2 skill and v3 requirement, but execution was created against v1 requirement and v1 skill; dashboard reports current coverage from historic pass. | Graph, QA | HIGH | **Addressed in existing ASER requirements**: evidence tiers and historical pins kept separate; run acceptance needs real executable tests. |
